The archival project was, in fact, an obsession of Leibniz’s, but why? What Leibniz saw was that the very nature of good governance cannot be separated from the keeping of archives in a secure and immutable fashion. What Leibniz could not see was that the archives should also be widely accessible, and that to achieve both these goals they must be decentralised.
